# Graph Report - repo  (2026-08-08)

## Corpus Check
- 4070 files · ~8,900,560 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 28054 nodes · 41397 edges · 3308 communities (1385 shown, 1923 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1494 edges (avg confidence: 0.71)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `9f349618`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
